                    isRunning = BypassController.Current.IsRunning,
                    zapretRunning = BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning,
                    proxyRunning = BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning
                });
            }
        }

        private void HandleExportLogs(JsonElement root)
        {
            var logText = root.GetProperty("text").GetString() ?? "";
            _mainWindow.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
            {
                var saveFileDialog = new Microsoft.Win32.SaveFileDialog
                {
                    Filter = "Текстовые файлы (*.txt)|*.txt|Все файлы (*.*)|*.*",
                    DefaultExt = ".txt",
                    FileName = $"Zapret_Log_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.txt"
                };

                if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() == true)
                {
                    File.WriteAllText(saveFileDialog.FileName, logText);
                    SendEvent("alert", new { title = "Экспорт завершен", message = "Лог событий успешно сохранен в файл!", isError = false });
                }
            });
        }

        private async Task HandleRunDiagnosticsAsync()
        {
            var report = await DiagnosticsEngine.RunFullDiagnosticsAsync((prog, text) =>
            {
                SendEvent("diagProgress", new { progress = Math.Round(prog * 100), status = text });
            });

            AudioHelper.PlaySuccess();
            SendEvent("diagResult", report);
        }

        private void HandleSaveSettings(JsonElement s)
        {
            var cur = SettingsManager.Current;

            if (s.TryGetProperty("autoStart", out var autoStart))
            {
                bool wantAuto = autoStart.GetBoolean();
                if (StartupHelper.IsAutoStartEnabled() != wantAuto)
                    StartupHelper.SetAutoStart(wantAuto);
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("startMinimized", out var sm)) cur.StartMinimized = sm.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("minimizeOnClose", out var moc)) cur.MinimizeOnClose = moc.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("notificationsEnabled", out var ne)) cur.NotificationsEnabled = ne.GetBoolean();
            if (s.TryGetProperty("focusMode", out var fm)) cur.FocusMode = fm.GetBoolean();

            if (s.TryGetProperty("compactMode", out var cm))
            {
                bool newCompact = cm.GetBoolean();
                if (cur.CompactMode != newCompact)
                {
                    cur.CompactMode = newCompact;
                    if (_mainWindow is MainWindow mw)
                        mw.AnimateWindowSize(newCompact);
                }
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("hardwareAcceleration", out var ha))
            {
                bool useGpu = ha.GetBoolean();
                if (cur.HardwareAcceleration != useGpu)
                {
                    cur.HardwareAcceleration = useGpu;
                    _mainWindow.Dispatcher.Invoke(() =>
                    {
                        RenderOptions.ProcessRenderMode = useGpu ? RenderMode.Default : RenderMode.SoftwareOnly;
                    });
                }
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("colorblindMode", out var cb))
            {
                bool newCb = cb.GetBoolean();
                if (cur.ColorblindMode != newCb)
                {
                    cur.ColorblindMode = newCb;
                    UIHelper.ApplyThemeColors();
                }
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("pingUrl", out var pu))
            {
                var urlStr = pu.GetString();
                cur.PingUrl = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(urlStr) ? AppConstants.DefaultPingUrl : urlStr.Trim();
            }

            if (s.TryGetProperty("statsUpdateInterval", out var sui))
                cur.StatsUpdateInterval = Math.Max(1, sui.GetInt32());

            if (s.TryGetProperty("autoRestartServices", out var ars))
                cur.AutoRestartServices = ars.GetBoolean();

            if (s.TryGetProperty("hostsSourceIndex", out var hsi))
                cur.HostsSourceIndex = Math.Clamp(hsi.GetInt32(), 0, 3);

            SettingsManager.Save();
        }
        public void Dispose()
        {
            _watcherDebounceCts?.Cancel();
            _watcherDebounceCts?.Dispose();
            _modsWatcher?.Dispose();
            _pendingConfirmTcs?.TrySetResult(false);
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ZapretManager.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class ZapretManager : IBypassService
    {
        private static readonly Regex _winwsCmdRegex = new($@"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}(?:\.exe)?[""']?\s*(.*)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _standardBatCmdRegex = new(@"^(@?echo|chcp|cd|pushd|popd|set|title|cls|color|mode|goto|call|if|for|pause|timeout|:)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _tokenSplitRegex = new(@"[\""].+?[\""]|[^ ]+", RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _fileParamRegex = new(@"^--(hostlist|hostlist-exclude|ipset|ipset-exclude|dpi-desync-fake-tls|dpi-desync-fake-quic|dpi-desync-fake-unknown-udp|dpi-desync-split-seqovl-pattern)=(.+)$", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.Compiled);
        private static readonly Regex _unexpandedVarRegex = new(@"%[a-zA-Z0-9_]+%", RegexOptions.Compiled);

        private readonly string _basePath;
        private Process? _process;
        private DateTime _lastCheckTime = DateTime.MinValue;
        private bool _cachedRunning = false;

        public event Action<string>? LogMessage;

        public ZapretManager()
        {
            _basePath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory);
        }

        public List<string> GetAvailableProfiles()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(_basePath))
                return new List<string> { "general.bat" };

            var list = Directory.GetFiles(_basePath, "*.bat")
                .Select(Path.GetFileName)
                .Where(name => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(name) &&
                              (name.StartsWith("general", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                               name.StartsWith("mod_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                .Cast<string>()
                .OrderBy(n => n)
                .ToList();

            return list.Count > 0 ? list : new List<string> { "general.bat" };
        }

        private string GetSourceDisplayName(string profileName)
        {
            if (profileName.StartsWith("mod_", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                var modId = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(profileName).Substring(4);
                var modJsonPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.ModsDirectory, "strategies", modId, "mod.json");
                if (File.Exists(modJsonPath))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var meta = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ModMetaData>(File.ReadAllText(modJsonPath), new JsonSerializerOptions { PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true });
                        if (meta != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(meta.Name))
                            return $"моде «{meta.Name}» ({profileName})";
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
                return $"моде «{modId}» ({profileName})";
            }
            return $"профиле «{profileName}»";
        }

        public static string ParseAndValidateBatLines(string[] lines, string sourceName, string zapretBasePath, bool checkReferencedFiles = true)
        {
            if (lines == null || lines.Length == 0 || lines.All(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace))
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName}: Файл стратегии пуст.");

            var arguments = "";
            var isReadingArgs = false;
            var winwsFound = false;
            var winwsStartLine = 0;

            for (var i = 0; i < lines.Length; i++)
            {
                var lineNum = i + 1;
                var rawLine = lines[i];
                var trimmed = rawLine.Trim();

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(trimmed) ||
                    trimmed.StartsWith("::") ||
                    trimmed.StartsWith("rem ", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    trimmed.Equals("rem", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (!isReadingArgs)
                {
                    if (trimmed.Contains(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        isReadingArgs = true;
                        winwsFound = true;
                        winwsStartLine = lineNum;

                        var match = _winwsCmdRegex.Match(trimmed);
                        trimmed = match.Success ? match.Groups[1].Value.Trim() : "";
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (!_standardBatCmdRegex.IsMatch(trimmed))
                            throw new Exception($"Синтаксическая ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНеизвестная команда «{trimmed}».\nОжидался вызов {AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe или стандартная инструкция Batch.");
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                if (isReadingArgs)
                {
                    var hasContinuation = trimmed.EndsWith("^");
                    var cleanLine = hasContinuation ? trimmed.Substring(0, trimmed.Length - 1).TrimEnd() : trimmed;
                    var quoteCount = cleanLine.Count(c => c == '\"');

                    if (quoteCount % 2 != 0)
                        throw new Exception($"Ошибка синтаксиса в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНе закрыта кавычка (\") в параметрах:\n«{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleanLine))
                    {
                        var tokens = _tokenSplitRegex.Matches(cleanLine)
                                          .Select(m => m.Value.Trim())
                                          .ToList();

                        foreach (var token in tokens)
                        {
                            if (!token.StartsWith("--") && !token.StartsWith("-") && !token.Equals("--new", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                throw new Exception($"Ошибка параметра в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nАргумент «{token}» не подходит для Zapret (winws).\nВсе параметры должны начинаться с «--» (например, --filter-tcp=80 или --dpi-desync=fake).\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                            if (token.StartsWith("---") || token == "--" || token == "-")
                                throw new Exception($"Ошибка синтаксиса в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nНекорректный формат флага «{token}».\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");

                            if (checkReferencedFiles && Directory.Exists(zapretBasePath))
                            {
                                var fileMatch = _fileParamRegex.Match(token);
                                if (fileMatch.Success)
                                {
                                    var paramName = fileMatch.Groups[1].Value;
                                    var rawFilePath = fileMatch.Groups[2].Value.Trim('\"', '\'');

                                    if (!rawFilePath.StartsWith("0x", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !rawFilePath.StartsWith("!"))
                                    {
                                        var resolvedPath = rawFilePath
                                            .Replace("%BIN%", @"bin\")
                                            .Replace("%LISTS%", @"lists\")
                                            .Replace("%~dp0", "");

                                        var fullCheckPath = Path.IsPathRooted(resolvedPath)
                                            ? resolvedPath
                                            : Path.Combine(zapretBasePath, resolvedPath);

                                        if (!File.Exists(fullCheckPath))
                                            throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {lineNum}]:\nВ параметре «--{paramName}» указан несуществующий файл «{rawFilePath}».\nСтрока: «{rawLine.Trim()}»");
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        arguments += " " + cleanLine;
                    }

                    if (!hasContinuation)
                        break;
                }
            }

            if (!winwsFound)
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка структуры в {sourceName}:\nВ батнике не найден вызов ядра «{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe». Этот скрипт не подходит для Zapret.");

            var (gfAll, gfTcp, gfUdp) = ServiceToolsManager.GetGameFilterSubstitutions();
            ServiceToolsManager.SyncGameFilterFile();

            arguments = arguments.Replace("%BIN%", @"bin\")
                                 .Replace("%LISTS%", @"lists\")
                                 .Replace("%~dp0", "")
                                 .Replace("%GameFilterTCP%", gfTcp)
                                 .Replace("%GameFilterUDP%", gfUdp)
                                 .Replace("%GameFilter%", gfAll)
                                 .Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(arguments))
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName} [Строка {winwsStartLine}]:\nКоманда «{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}» вызвана без параметров фильтрации.");

            var unexpandedVar = _unexpandedVarRegex.Match(arguments);
            if (unexpandedVar.Success)
                throw new Exception($"Ошибка в {sourceName}:\nОбнаружена неизвестная переменная «{unexpandedVar.Value}», которая не поддерживается в прямом запуске Zapret.");

            return arguments;
        }

        public void Start(string profileName)
        {
            Stop();

            var batFilePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, profileName);
            var sourceName = GetSourceDisplayName(profileName);

            if (!File.Exists(batFilePath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Конфигурационный файл не найден в {sourceName} по пути: {batFilePath}");

            var lines = File.ReadAllLines(batFilePath);
            var arguments = ParseAndValidateBatLines(lines, sourceName, _basePath, checkReferencedFiles: true);

            var exePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, $"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe");
            var binExePath = Path.Combine(_basePath, "bin", $"{AppConstants.ZapretProcessName}.exe");

            if (!File.Exists(exePath) && File.Exists(binExePath))
                exePath = binExePath;

            if (!File.Exists(exePath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Не найден исполняемый файл ядра по пути: {exePath}. Запустите проверку обновлений в настройках.");

            var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
            {
                FileName = exePath,
                Arguments = arguments,
                UseShellExecute = false,
                CreateNoWindow = true,
                RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                RedirectStandardError = true,
                WorkingDirectory = _basePath
            };

            _process = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };

            var errorOutputs = new ConcurrentQueue<string>();

            _process.OutputDataReceived += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data))
                    return;

                var line = e.Data;
                var noise = new[]
                {
                    "Loading hostlist", "loading plain text list", "Loaded",
                    "user defined desync profile", "github version", "windivert initialized"
                };

                if (noise.Any(n => line.Contains(n, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                    return;

                LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Zapret] {line}");
            };

            _process.ErrorDataReceived += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.Data))
                {
                    errorOutputs.Enqueue(e.Data);
                    LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Zapret ERR] {e.Data}");
                }
            };

            _process.Start();
            _process.BeginOutputReadLine();
            _process.BeginErrorReadLine();

            if (_process.WaitForExit(350))
            {
                var errDetails = !errorOutputs.IsEmpty
                    ? string.Join(" | ", errorOutputs)
                    : $"Код завершения: {_process.ExitCode}";

                _cachedRunning = false;
                throw new Exception($"Ядро Zapret отвергло конфигурацию в {sourceName}!\nПричина от движка winws: {errDetails}");
            }

            _cachedRunning = true;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public void Stop()
        {
            try
            {
                if (_process != null)
                {
                    if (!_process.HasExited)
                        _process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                    _process.Dispose();
                    _process = null;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка остановки процесса Zapret: {ex.Message}");
            }

            ProcessHelper.KillProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
            _cachedRunning = false;
            _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public bool IsRunning
        {
            get
            {
                try
                {
                    if (_process != null && !_process.HasExited)
                        return true;
                }
                catch { }

                if ((DateTime.UtcNow - _lastCheckTime).TotalSeconds < 2.5)
                    return _cachedRunning;

                _cachedRunning = ProcessHelper.IsProcessRunning(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
                _lastCheckTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
                return _cachedRunning;
            }
        }
    }
}

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Core\ZapretScanner.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Timers;

namespace ZapretGUI.Core
{
    public class ZapretScanner
    {
        [DllImport("user32.dll")]
        private static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);
        private const int SW_HIDE = 0;

        private Process? _scanProcess;
        private CancellationTokenSource? _scanCts;
        private readonly List<string> _topConfigs = new List<string>();

        public event Action<string>? LogMessage;
        public event Action<string, int, int>? ConfigTested; // fileName, okCount, totalCount
        public event Action<List<string>>? ScanCompleted;
        public event Action<Exception>? ScanFailed;

        public bool IsScanning => _scanProcess != null && !_scanProcess.HasExited;

        public async Task StartScanAsync()
        {
            if (IsScanning) return;

            _topConfigs.Clear();
            _scanCts = new CancellationTokenSource();

            using var hideTimer = new System.Timers.Timer(100);
            hideTimer.Elapsed += (s, ev) =>
            {
                foreach (var p in Process.GetProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName))
                {
                    if (p.MainWindowHandle != IntPtr.Zero)
                        ShowWindow(p.MainWindowHandle, SW_HIDE);
                }
            };
            hideTimer.Start();

            try
            {
                var scriptPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, AppConstants.CoreFilesDirectory, "utils", "test zapret.ps1");

                if (!File.Exists(scriptPath))
                    throw new FileNotFoundException("Скрипт тестирования не найден по пути: " + scriptPath);

                var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = "powershell.exe",
                    Arguments = $"-NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"{scriptPath}\"",
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardInput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true,
                    StandardOutputEncoding = System.Text.Encoding.UTF8
                };

                _scanProcess = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };
                _scanProcess.Start();

                await _scanProcess.StandardInput.WriteLineAsync("1");
                await _scanProcess.StandardInput.WriteLineAsync("1");
                _scanProcess.StandardInput.Close();

                await Task.Run(async () =>
                {
                    while (!_scanProcess.StandardOutput.EndOfStream)
                    {
                        if (_scanCts.Token.IsCancellationRequested) break;

                        var line = await _scanProcess.StandardOutput.ReadLineAsync();
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                            continue;

                        line = Regex.Replace(line, @"\x1B\[[0-9;]*[a-zA-Z]", "");

                        string? simpleLog = SimplifyLogLine(line);
                        if (simpleLog != null)
                            LogMessage?.Invoke($"[Auto] {simpleLog}");
                    }

                    if (!_scanProcess.HasExited)
                        _scanProcess.WaitForExit();

                }, _scanCts.Token);

                if (_scanCts.Token.IsCancellationRequested)
                    LogMessage?.Invoke("🛑 Сканирование было прервано пользователем.");
                else
                    ScanCompleted?.Invoke(new List<string>(_topConfigs));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ScanFailed?.Invoke(ex);
            }
            finally
            {
                hideTimer.Stop();
            }
        }

        public void CancelScan()
        {
            _scanCts?.Cancel();
            try
            {
                if (_scanProcess != null && !_scanProcess.HasExited)
                {
                    _scanProcess.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
                    _scanProcess.Dispose();
                    _scanProcess = null;
                }
            }
            catch { }

            ProcessHelper.KillProcessesByName(AppConstants.ZapretProcessName);
        }

        private string? SimplifyLogLine(string line)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line))
                return null;

            var progressMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"\[(\d+/\d+)\]\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (progressMatch.Success)
            {
                var progress = progressMatch.Groups[1].Value;
                var configName = progressMatch.Groups[2].Value.Trim();
                return $"[{progress}] 🔍 Анализ профиля: {configName}...";
            }

            if (line.Contains("=== ANALYTICS ==="))
                return "📊 Сводка результатов тестирования:";

            var statsMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)\s*:\s*(?:HTTP\s*)?OK:\s*(\d+).*?(?:ERR|FAIL):\s*(\d+)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
            if (statsMatch.Success)
            {
                var batName = statsMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                var okStr = statsMatch.Groups[2].Value;
                var errStr = statsMatch.Groups[3].Value;

                int.TryParse(okStr, out int ok);
                int.TryParse(errStr, out int err);
                ConfigTested?.Invoke(batName, ok, ok + err);

                if (err == 0 && ok > 0)
                {
                    if (!_topConfigs.Contains(batName)) _topConfigs.Add(batName);
                    return $"      ✨ {batName} -> Работает идеально (Успешно: {okStr})";
                }
                else
                    return $"      ⚠️ {batName} -> Есть блокировки (Успешно: {okStr}, Ошибок: {errStr})";
            }

            if (line.Contains("Best config:"))
            {
                var bestMatch = Regex.Match(line, @"Best config:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\(\)\s]+\.bat)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
                if (bestMatch.Success)
                {
                    var best = bestMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                    if (_topConfigs.Contains(best)) _topConfigs.Remove(best);
                    _topConfigs.Insert(0, best);
                }
                return null;
            }

            return null;
        }
    }
}

                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="0" Text="&#xE8A7;" FontFamily="Segoe MDL2 Assets" Foreground="#999999" FontSize="14" Margin="0,0,10,0" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="1" Text="Открыть панель управления" Foreground="#E0E0E0" FontSize="12.5" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="2" Text="&#xE76C;" FontFamily="Segoe MDL2 Assets" Foreground="#555555" FontSize="10" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>
                </Grid>
            </Button>

            <Button x:Name="BtnExit" Style="{StaticResource TrayDangerItemStyle}" Click="BtnExit_Click">
                <Grid>
                    <Grid.ColumnDefinitions>
                        <ColumnDefinition Width="Auto"/>
                        <ColumnDefinition Width="*"/>

                    <TextBlock Grid.Column="1" Text="Полностью закрыть" Foreground="#F44336" FontSize="12.5" FontWeight="SemiBold" VerticalAlignment="Center"/>

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// ФАЙЛ: Views\TrayMenuWindow.xaml.cs
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using ZapretGUI.Core;

namespace ZapretGUI.Views
{
    public partial class TrayMenuWindow : Window
    {
        private bool _isRunning = false;

        public TrayMenuWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
            TxtTrayVersion.Text = AppConstants.AppVersion;
            BypassController.Current.NetMonitor.StatsUpdated += OnNetStatsUpdated;
        }

        private void OnNetStatsUpdated(double rxMbps, double txMbps)
        {
            if (!IsVisible) return;

            Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() =>
            {
                TxtDownloadSpeed.Text = $"{rxMbps:0.0} Мбит/с";
                TxtUploadSpeed.Text = $"{txMbps:0.0} Мбит/с";
                SyncStatus();
            });
        }

        public void PlayPopupAnimation()
        {
            var duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(220));
            var ease = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut };

            MainBorder.BeginAnimation(UIElement.OpacityProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0.0, 1.0, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
            MenuScale.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0.88, 1.0, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
            MenuScale.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0.95, 1.0, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
            MenuTranslate.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.YProperty, new DoubleAnimation(12.0, 0.0, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
        }

        public void RefreshState()
        {
            SyncStatus();
            CheckPingAsync();
        }

        private void Window_Deactivated(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            this.Hide();
        }

        private async void CheckPingAsync()
        {
            PingText.Text = "...";
            PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#888888");

            try
            {
                var pingMs = await BypassController.Current.CheckPingAsync();

                if (pingMs >= 0)
                {
                    PingText.Text = $"{pingMs} мс";

                    if (pingMs < 80)
                        PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetSuccessBrush();
                    else if (pingMs < 150)
                        PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#FF8C00");
                    else
                        PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetErrorBrush();
                }
                else
                {
                    PingText.Text = "недоступен";
                    PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetErrorBrush();
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.WriteLine($"Ошибка проверки пинга из трея: {ex.Message}");
                PingText.Text = "ошибка";
                PingText.Foreground = UIHelper.GetErrorBrush();
            }
        }

        private void SyncStatus()
        {
            bool zapretRun = BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning;
            bool proxyRun = BypassController.Current.TgProxy.IsRunning;
            _isRunning = zapretRun || proxyRun;

            var onlineBrush = UIHelper.GetSuccessBrush();
            var offlineBrush = UIHelper.GetErrorBrush();

            DotZapret.Fill = zapretRun ? onlineBrush : offlineBrush;
            DotProxy.Fill = proxyRun ? onlineBrush : offlineBrush;

            TxtActiveProfile.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName)
                ? "general.bat"
                : SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName;

            if (zapretRun && proxyRun)
                TxtSubStatus.Text = "Zapret + TgWsProxy активны";
            else if (zapretRun)
                TxtSubStatus.Text = "Обход Zapret активен";
            else if (proxyRun)
                TxtSubStatus.Text = "Прокси Telegram активен";
            else
                TxtSubStatus.Text = "Службы остановлены";

            TxtSubStatus.Foreground = _isRunning
                ? UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#4CAF50")
                : UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#888888");

            UpdateToggleButtonVisuals();
        }

        private void BtnTogglePower_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            _isRunning = !_isRunning;
            UpdateToggleButtonVisuals();

            if (System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow is MainWindow mainWindow)
                mainWindow.ToggleBypass();

            Dispatcher.InvokeAsync(async () =>
            {
                await System.Threading.Tasks.Task.Delay(450);
                SyncStatus();
            });
        }

        private void BtnRefreshPing_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            CheckPingAsync();
        }

        private async void BtnRestartBypass_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            TxtSubStatus.Text = "Перезапуск служб...";
            TxtSubStatus.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#FF8C00");

            if (System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow is MainWindow mainWindow)
            {
                await mainWindow.RestartBypassAsync();
            }
            SyncStatus();
        }

        private void UpdateToggleButtonVisuals()
        {
            BtnTogglePower.ApplyTemplate();

            var powerIcon = (System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock)BtnTogglePower.Template.FindName("PowerIcon", BtnTogglePower);
            var powerText = (System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock)BtnTogglePower.Template.FindName("PowerText", BtnTogglePower);

            if (_isRunning)
            {
                BtnTogglePower.Background = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#2A1215");
                BtnTogglePower.BorderBrush = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#D13438");

                if (powerIcon != null)
                {
                    powerIcon.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#F44336");
                    powerIcon.Text = "\xE71A";
                }
                if (powerText != null)
                    powerText.Text = "Остановить обход";
            }
            else
            {
                BtnTogglePower.Background = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#132615");
                BtnTogglePower.BorderBrush = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#107C10");

                if (powerIcon != null)
                {
                    powerIcon.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#4CAF50");
                    powerIcon.Text = "\xE7E8";
                }
                if (powerText != null)
                    powerText.Text = "Включить обход";
            }
        }

        private void BtnOpenApp_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            if (System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow != null)
            {
                System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow.Show();
                System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow.WindowState = WindowState.Normal;
                System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow.Activate();
            }
            this.Hide();
        }

        private void BtnExit_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            BypassController.Current.NetMonitor.StatsUpdated -= OnNetStatsUpdated;
            System.Windows.Application.Current.Shutdown();
        }
        private void BtnPrevProfile_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _ = CycleProfileAsync(-1);
        }

        private void BtnNextProfile_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            _ = CycleProfileAsync(1);
        }

        private async System.Threading.Tasks.Task CycleProfileAsync(int dir)
        {
            AudioHelper.PlayClick();
            var profiles = BypassController.Current.Zapret.GetAvailableProfiles();
            if (profiles.Count == 0) return;

            var current = SettingsManager.Current.SelectedProfileName ?? "general.bat";
            var idx = profiles.FindIndex(p => string.Equals(p, current, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            var nextIdx = idx < 0 ? 0 : (idx + dir + profiles.Count) % profiles.Count;
            var nextProfile = profiles[nextIdx];

            TxtActiveProfile.Text = nextProfile;

            if (System.Windows.Application.Current.MainWindow is MainWindow mainWindow)
            {
                if (BypassController.Current.Zapret.IsRunning)
                {
                    TxtSubStatus.Text = $"Запуск {nextProfile}...";
                    TxtSubStatus.Foreground = UIHelper.GetBrushFromHex("#FF8C00");
                }
                await mainWindow.SelectProfileFromTrayAsync(nextProfile);
            }
            SyncStatus();
        }
    }
}

							</label>
						</div>

						<!-- ШАГ 3: ДОПОЛНИТЕЛЬНЫЕ НАСТРОЙКИ -->
						<div id="wiz-step-3" class="wizard-step flex-col w-full space-y-3 max-w-xl mx-auto">
							<div>
								<h3 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white">Персонализация и поведение</h3>
								<p class="text-sm text-[#888888] mt-1">Эти параметры можно в любой момент изменить в разделе «Настройки».</p>
							</div>

							<label class="wiz-option-card selected" id="wiz-card-autostart">
								<div>
									<div class="text-sm font-bold text-white">Запускать вместе с Windows</div>

При включении тумблера такой мод сразу появляется в списке конфигов на Главной вкладке с префиксом <code class="text-white bg-[#222222] px-1 rounded">mod_</code>.

									</div>
									<div>
										<b class="text-white">• Валидатор синтаксиса:</b> Кнопка <span class="text-brand">«Проверить синтаксис»</span> проверяет кавычки, флаги и существование файлов списков.
									</div>

									</div>
									<div>
										<b class="text-white">• Умный импорт:</b> Нажмите <b class="text-brand">«Импорт (.zip)»</b> в шапке — программа сама распознает тип мода и защитит от конфликта ID.

// =========================================================================
// 1. SPLASH WINDOW (МАТРИЧНЫЙ ТЕРМИНАЛ ЗАГРУЗКИ)
// =========================================================================
const SPLASH_LINES = [
    "[ SYSTEM ] ADHD Engine initiated. Focus level: 4%",
    "[ OK     ] Coffee levels detected: Critical but acceptable.",
    "[ INFO   ] Inspecting deep internet blockages...",
    "[ SEARCH ] Looking for winws.exe...",
    "[ SEARCH ] Looking for tgwsproxy.exe...",
    "[ SEARCH ] Getting distracted by a random Wikipedia article...",
    "[ FIX    ] Focus restored! Files found.",
    "[ WARN   ] WinDivert64.sys loading... Please don't close this window or look at shiny objects.",
    "[ OK     ] WinDivert driver injected successfully.",
    "[ SOCKS5 ] tgwsproxy spinning up on port 1080...",
    "[ PACKET ] Fragmentation: ENABLED",
    "[ PACKET ] HTTP Host Spoofing: ACTIVE",
    "[ PACKET ] HTTPS SNI Trickery: ACTIVE",
    "[ BYPASS ] Shaking hands with forbidden packets...",
    "[ BYPASS ] Harder... Faster... Better... Stronger...",
    "[ OK     ] DPI bypassed! Access to the matrix granted.",
    "[ STATUS ] BYPASS ACTIVE. GO WATCH YOUTUBE IN 4K NOW!",
    "==========================================================================",
    "  ____   _    ____   ____   _____ _____   ______ ____   ____     ",
    " /_  /  / |  / __ \\ / __ \\ / ___//__  /   / ____// __ \\ / __ \\    ",
    "  / /  / /| |/ /_/ // /_/ // __/    / /   / /_   / / / // /_/ /    ",
    " / /_ / ___// ____// _, _// /___   / /   / __/  / /_/ // _, _/     ",
    "/____/_/  |_/_/   /_/ |_|/_____/  /_/   /_/     \\____//_/ |_|      ",
    "                                                                    ",
    "            ___     ____   _   _   ____                             ",
    "           /   |   / __ \\ / / / / / __ \\                            ",
    "          / /| |  / / / // /_/ / / / / /                            ",
    "         / ___ | / /_/ // __  / / /_/ /                             ",
    "        /_/  |_|/_____//_/ /_/ /_____/                              ",
    "=========================================================================="
];

let splashSkipped = false;
async function runSplashSequence() {
    const term = document.getElementById('splash-terminal');
    for (let i = 0; i < SPLASH_LINES.length; i++) {
        if (splashSkipped) return;
        const line = SPLASH_LINES[i];
        term.textContent += line + '\n';
        term.scrollTop = term.scrollHeight;

        let delay = Math.floor(Math.random() * 80) + 15;
        if (line.includes('Wikipedia')) delay = 900;
        else if (line.includes('___') || line.includes('===') || line.includes('/_')) delay = 25;
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, delay));
    }
    if (!splashSkipped) {
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 700));
        skipSplash();
    }
}

function skipSplash() {
    splashSkipped = true;
    const el = document.getElementById('splash-overlay');
    el.style.opacity = '0';
    el.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    setTimeout(() => el.style.display = 'none', 450);
}

// =========================================================================
// 2. WIZARD WINDOW 2.0 + ПРОЦЕДУРНЫЙ 3D ASCII ДВИЖОК (TORUS, SHIELD, HELIX, CUBE)
// =========================================================================
let wizAsciiTimer = null;
let wizAsciiShape = 0; // 0: Torus, 1: Octahedron Shield, 2: DNA Network Helix, 3: Cyber Cube
let wizPitch = 0.45;   // Наклон вверх-вниз (вокруг оси X)
let wizYaw = 0.4;      // Поворот влево-вправо (вокруг оси Y)
let wizVelPitch = 0;   // Инерция по вертикали
let wizVelYaw = 0;     // Инерция по горизонтали
let wizIsDragging = false;
let wizLastMouseX = 0, wizLastMouseY = 0;
let wizHyperSpeed = false;

const ASCII_MODELS = [
    'MODEL: TORUS_REACTOR.3D',
    'MODEL: SHIELD_OCTA.3D',
    'MODEL: NET_HELIX.3D',
    'MODEL: HYPER_CUBE.3D'
];

function startWizAsciiEngine() {
    if (wizAsciiTimer) return;
    wizAsciiTimer = setInterval(renderWizAsciiFrame, 33); // ~30 FPS
}

function stopWizAsciiEngine() {
    if (wizAsciiTimer) {
        clearInterval(wizAsciiTimer);
        wizAsciiTimer = null;
    }
}

function cycleWizAsciiShape() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    wizAsciiShape = (wizAsciiShape + 1) % 4;
    const lbl = document.getElementById('wiz-ascii-model-name');
    if (lbl) lbl.innerText = ASCII_MODELS[wizAsciiShape];
}

// Захват 3D-модели только при зажатии ЛКМ
function onWizAsciiMouseDown(e) {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;
    wizIsDragging = true;
    wizLastMouseX = e.clientX;
    wizLastMouseY = e.clientY;
    document.getElementById('wiz-left-panel')?.classList.add('is-dragging');
}

window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!wizIsDragging) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - wizLastMouseX;
    const dy = e.clientY - wizLastMouseY;
    wizLastMouseX = e.clientX;
    wizLastMouseY = e.clientY;

    // Крутим строго в сторону движения мыши:
    // Мышь вправо (dx > 0) -> передняя грань идет вправо (Yaw+)
    // Мышь вниз (dy > 0)   -> передняя грань идет вниз (Pitch+)
    wizYaw += dx * 0.013;
    wizPitch += dy * 0.013;

    // Запоминаем скорость для плавной инерции после отпускания мыши
    wizVelYaw = dx * 0.005;
    wizVelPitch = dy * 0.005;
});

window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    if (wizIsDragging) {
        wizIsDragging = false;
        document.getElementById('wiz-left-panel')?.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    }
});

function renderWizAsciiFrame() {
    const stage = document.getElementById('wiz-ascii-stage');
    if (!stage) return;

    const W = 58, H = 30;
    const output = new Array(W * H).fill(' ');
    const zbuffer = new Array(W * H).fill(0);
    const chars = '.,-~:;=!*#$@';

    if (!wizIsDragging) {
        const speedMult = wizHyperSpeed ? 2.8 : 1.0;
        // Плавное авто-вращение + затухающая инерция после броска мышкой
        wizYaw += (0.032 * speedMult) + wizVelYaw;
        wizPitch += (0.014 * speedMult) + wizVelPitch;
        wizVelYaw *= 0.90;
        wizVelPitch *= 0.90;
    }

    const cosY = Math.cos(wizYaw), sinY = Math.sin(wizYaw);
    const cosX = Math.cos(wizPitch), sinX = Math.sin(wizPitch);

    function plotPoint(x, y, z, nx, ny, nz) {
        // 1. Вращение вокруг вертикальной оси Y (Yaw — влево/вправо за мышкой)
        const x1 = x * cosY - z * sinY;
        const z1 = x * sinY + z * cosY;
        const y1 = y;

        // 2. Вращение вокруг горизонтальной оси X (Pitch — вверх/вниз за мышкой)
        const y2 = y1 * cosX - z1 * sinX;
        const z2 = y1 * sinX + z1 * cosX;
        const x2 = x1;

        const depth = z2 + 5.0;
        if (depth <= 0.5) return;
        const ooz = 1 / depth;

        // Экранные координаты: X вправо, Y вверх (в консоль вниз)
        const xp = Math.floor(W / 2 + 34 * ooz * x2);
        const yp = Math.floor(H / 2 - 17 * ooz * y2);

        if (xp < 0 || xp >= W || yp < 0 || yp >= H) return;
        const idx = xp + W * yp;

        if (ooz > zbuffer[idx]) {
            zbuffer[idx] = ooz;

            // Поворачиваем нормали для реалистичного блика света
            const nx1 = nx * cosY - nz * sinY;
            const nz1 = nx * sinY + nz * cosY;
            const ny2 = ny * cosX - nz1 * sinX;
            const nz2 = ny * sinX + nz1 * cosX;

            const lum = (nx1 * 0.35 + ny2 * 0.45 - nz2 * 0.75 + 1) * 0.5;
            const cIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(chars.length - 1, Math.floor(lum * chars.length)));
            output[idx] = chars[cIdx];
        }
    }

    if (wizAsciiShape === 0) {
        // 0. Классический 3D Тор (Donut Reactor)
        const R1 = 0.85, R2 = 1.75;
        for (let theta = 0; theta < 6.28; theta += 0.13) {
            const cost = Math.cos(theta), sint = Math.sin(theta);
            for (let phi = 0; phi < 6.28; phi += 0.055) {
                const cosp = Math.cos(phi), sinp = Math.sin(phi);
                const circleX = R2 + R1 * cost;
                plotPoint(
                    circleX * cosp,
                    circleX * sinp,
                    R1 * sint,
                    cost * cosp,
                    cost * sinp,
                    sint
                );
            }
        }
    } else if (wizAsciiShape === 1) {
        // 1. 3D Кристалл / Октаэдр защиты (Privacy Shield)
        const S = 2.05;
        const verts = [
            [0, S, 0], [0, -S, 0], [S, 0, 0], [-S, 0, 0], [0, 0, S], [0, 0, -S]
        ];
        const faces = [
            [0, 4, 2], [0, 2, 5], [0, 5, 3], [0, 3, 4],
            [1, 2, 4], [1, 5, 2], [1, 3, 5], [1, 4, 3]
        ];
        faces.forEach(([i0, i1, i2]) => {
            const v0 = verts[i0], v1 = verts[i1], v2 = verts[i2];
            const nx = (v0[0] + v1[0] + v2[0]) / 3;
            const ny = (v0[1] + v1[1] + v2[1]) / 3;
            const nz = (v0[2] + v1[2] + v2[2]) / 3;
            for (let u = 0; u <= 1; u += 0.07) {
                for (let v = 0; u + v <= 1; v += 0.07) {
                    const w = 1 - u - v;
                    plotPoint(
                        u * v0[0] + v * v1[0] + w * v2[0],
                        u * v0[1] + v * v1[1] + w * v2[1],
                        u * v0[2] + v * v1[2] + w * v2[2],
                        nx, ny, nz
                    );
                }
            }
        });
    } else if (wizAsciiShape === 2) {
        // 2. Двойная сетевая спираль (Network DNA Helix)
        for (let t = -2.2; t <= 2.2; t += 0.05) {
            const angle = t * 2.2;
            const r = 1.35;
            const x1 = r * Math.cos(angle), z1 = r * Math.sin(angle);
            const x2 = -x1, z2 = -z1;

            for (let a = 0; a < 6.28; a += 0.7) {
                const cr = 0.24;
                plotPoint(x1 + cr * Math.cos(a), t, z1 + cr * Math.sin(a), Math.cos(a), 0, Math.sin(a));
                plotPoint(x2 + cr * Math.cos(a), t, z2 + cr * Math.sin(a), -Math.cos(a), 0, -Math.sin(a));
            }
            if (Math.abs((t * 10) % 4) < 0.5) {
                for (let s = -1; s <= 1; s += 0.11) {
                    plotPoint(x1 * s, t, z1 * s, 0, 1, 0);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        // 3. 3D Кибер-Куб (Hyper Cube)
        const C = 1.4;
        for (let u = -C; u <= C; u += 0.12) {
            for (let v = -C; v <= C; v += 0.12) {
                plotPoint(u, v, C, 0, 0, 1);
                plotPoint(u, v, -C, 0, 0, -1);
                plotPoint(u, C, v, 0, 1, 0);
                plotPoint(u, -C, v, 0, -1, 0);
                plotPoint(C, u, v, 1, 0, 0);
                plotPoint(-C, u, v, -1, 0, 0);
            }
        }
    }

    let lines = '';
    for (let r = 0; r < H; r++) {
        lines += output.slice(r * W, (r + 1) * W).join('') + '\n';
    }
    stage.textContent = lines;
}

function onWizardOptionChange() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    const zap = document.getElementById('wiz-chk-zapret').checked;
    const prx = document.getElementById('wiz-chk-proxy').checked;
    const aut = document.getElementById('wiz-chk-autostart').checked;
    const foc = document.getElementById('wiz-chk-focus').checked;
    const cb = document.getElementById('wiz-chk-colorblind').checked;

    document.getElementById('wiz-card-zapret')?.classList.toggle('selected', zap);
    document.getElementById('wiz-card-proxy')?.classList.toggle('selected', prx);
    document.getElementById('wiz-card-autostart')?.classList.toggle('selected', aut);
    document.getElementById('wiz-card-focus')?.classList.toggle('selected', foc);
    document.getElementById('wiz-card-colorblind')?.classList.toggle('selected', cb);

    // Живое превью цветовой слепоты прямо в мастере
    document.body.classList.toggle('colorblind-mode', cb);

    const sumZap = document.getElementById('wiz-sum-zapret');
    const sumPrx = document.getElementById('wiz-sum-proxy');
    const sumAut = document.getElementById('wiz-sum-auto');
    const sumThm = document.getElementById('wiz-sum-theme');

    if (sumZap) { sumZap.innerText = zap ? 'ВКЛ' : 'ВЫКЛ'; sumZap.className = zap ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#777777]'; }
    if (sumPrx) { sumPrx.innerText = prx ? 'ВКЛ' : 'ВЫКЛ'; sumPrx.className = prx ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#777777]'; }
    if (sumAut) { sumAut.innerText = aut ? 'ВКЛ' : 'ВЫКЛ'; sumAut.className = aut ? 'text-brand' : 'text-[#777777]'; }
    if (sumThm) { sumThm.innerText = cb ? 'Сине-оранж.' : 'Стандарт'; }
}

function openWizardModal() {
    wizardStep = 0;
    wizardFinished = false;
    wizHyperSpeed = false;
    const btnNext = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-next');
    if (btnNext) {
        btnNext.disabled = false;
        btnNext.style.opacity = '1';
    }
    onWizardOptionChange();
    updateWizardUI();
    startWizAsciiEngine();
    document.getElementById('wizard-overlay').classList.add('open');
}

function updateWizardUI() {
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        document.getElementById('wiz-step-' + i).classList.toggle('active', i === wizardStep);
        const stepInd = document.getElementById('wstep-ind-' + i);
        if (stepInd) {
            if (i < wizardStep) stepInd.className = 'wiz-stepper-item done';
            else if (i === wizardStep) stepInd.className = 'wiz-stepper-item active';
            else stepInd.className = 'wiz-stepper-item';
        }
    }

    // Автоматически меняем 3D ASCII-модель под тему текущего шага
    wizAsciiShape = wizardStep < 4 ? wizardStep : 0;
    wizHyperSpeed = (wizardStep === 4 && !wizardFinished);
    const modelLbl = document.getElementById('wiz-ascii-model-name');
    if (modelLbl) modelLbl.innerText = ASCII_MODELS[wizAsciiShape];

    const dots = document.getElementById('wiz-dots');
    dots.innerHTML = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(i =>
        `<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${i === wizardStep ? 'bg-[#107C10] scale-125' : 'bg-[#333333]'}"></span>`
    ).join('');

    const btnBack = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-back');
    const btnNext = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-next');
    const btnSkip = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-skip');

    if (wizardStep === 0) {
        btnNext.innerText = 'Начать настройку →';
        btnBack.classList.add('hidden');
        btnSkip.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else if (wizardStep === 3) {
        btnNext.innerText = 'Установить и применить →';
        btnBack.classList.remove('hidden');
        btnSkip.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else if (wizardStep === 4) {
        btnNext.innerText = 'Завершить';
        btnBack.classList.add('hidden');
        btnSkip.classList.add('hidden');
    } else {
        btnNext.innerText = 'Далее →';
        btnBack.classList.remove('hidden');
        btnSkip.classList.remove('hidden');
    }
}

function wizardPrevStep() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    if (wizardStep > 0 && wizardStep < 4) {
        wizardStep--;
        updateWizardUI();
    }
}

function wizardNextStep() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    if (wizardStep === 3) {
        wizardStep = 4;
        updateWizardUI();

        const btnNext = document.getElementById('wiz-btn-next');
        btnNext.disabled = true;
        btnNext.style.opacity = '0.5';
        document.getElementById('wiz-console-log').innerHTML = '';
        document.getElementById('wiz-finish-bar').style.width = '30%';
        const pct = document.getElementById('wiz-finish-pct');
        if (pct) pct.innerText = '30%';

        sendCmd('runWizardSetup', {
            useZapret: document.getElementById('wiz-chk-zapret').checked,
            useTgProxy: document.getElementById('wiz-chk-proxy').checked,
            autoStart: document.getElementById('wiz-chk-autostart').checked,
            focusMode: document.getElementById('wiz-chk-focus').checked,
            colorblind: document.getElementById('wiz-chk-colorblind').checked
        });
    } else if (wizardStep === 4) {
        if (wizardFinished) {
            stopWizAsciiEngine();
            document.getElementById('wizard-overlay').classList.remove('open');
        }
    } else if (wizardStep < 3) {
        wizardStep++;
        updateWizardUI();
    }
}

function skipWizard() {
    sendCmd('playClick');
    stopWizAsciiEngine();
    sendCmd('skipWizard');
    document.getElementById('wizard-overlay').classList.remove('open');
}

function appendWizardLog(time, message, isProgress) {
    const box = document.getElementById('wiz-console-log');
    const text = `[${time}] ${message}`;

    const pctMatch = message.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)%/);
    if (pctMatch) {
        const val = Math.min(98, Math.max(30, Math.round(parseFloat(pctMatch[1]))));
        document.getElementById('wiz-finish-bar').style.width = `${val}%`;
        const pctEl = document.getElementById('wiz-finish-pct');
        if (pctEl) pctEl.innerText = `${val}%`;
    }

    if (isProgress && lastWizProgressEl) {
        lastWizProgressEl.innerText = text;
        return;
    }

    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.innerText = text;
    box.appendChild(div);
    box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    lastWizProgressEl = isProgress ? div : null;
}

        compactMode: false,
